# SHIBUSDT #89469 near-TP continuation investigation Г?" 2026-10-10

- Status: investigation approved; implementation is explicitly not approved.
  This workstream must not alter or delay FIX-153 release work.
- Hypothesis to test: a breached profit lock close to target may sometimes
  continue when current market structure and momentum remain supportive.
- Important counter-hypothesis: `1h=NEUTRAL` is not positive continuation
  evidence merely because 1m/5m are `WATCH`. Relaxing that guard on distance
  to TP alone could create false holds in weak or sideways markets.
- Required evidence before a policy change: use historical Replay/production
  evidence to compare the current exit rule against a hypothetical bounded
  hold using only information available at the breach. Segment results by
  1h/5m context, regime, trend structure, momentum, retracement depth and
  target-progress band. Evaluate TP hit rate, retained profit, adverse move,
  drawdown, and false-hold frequency. No post-exit price may inform entry or
  exit eligibility.
- Rejected approach: do not authorize continuation from `WATCH/WATCH/NEUTRAL`
  or near-TP distance alone.
- Code-review refinement: `PositionExitPolicy` creates a sharp tier boundary:
  with supportive 5m/current evidence, 1h `WATCH` can hold a breached lock but
  1h `NEUTRAL` falls through to `PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT`. Existing FIX-11T near-TP
  protection arms at 90% target progress and is evaluated after the ordinary
  lock-breach exit, so it could not affect #89469 at 84.28% progress.
- Fee-model refinement: `WalletAutoExecutionService` writes `fee_usdt=0` for
  paper BUY, SELL, and near-TP partial-harvest executions. Any policy Replay
  comparison must add an explicit fee/slippage model before claiming protected
  gross profit is a net gain.
- Approved experiment protocol from independent review: Production policy is
  unchanged. Compare (A) existing immediate lock exit, (B) a one-window,
  near-TP continuation using existing time-safe structure/momentum/context
  calculations, and (C) a volatility-adjusted tighter trailing exit. Use a
  pre-registered 85% TP-progress primary threshold; #89469 at 84.28% does not
  qualify and must not drive threshold selection. Require non-bearish 5m/1h,
  a fee-adjusted protected floor with modeled adverse fills, explicit numeric
  momentum/retracement definitions, no timer resets or repeated extensions,
  and strictly as-of-breach evidence. Evaluate net expectancy, drawdown,
  false-hold/adverse-move rate, and include later-decline counterexamples;
  reserve an out-of-sample period before any implementation decision.
- Final protocol controls: calculate TP progress only from immutable original
  entry and original TP; exclude and report zero/negative/invalid target
  distance. Every A/B/C candidate must use identical historical data,
  evaluation periods, and fee/slippage assumptions. Candidate B must model an
  intrawindow price path sufficiently to determine which of its emergency
  floor or target was reached first; candle high/low alone is not evidence of
  that order. Pre-register the minimum sample size, required net-expectancy
  improvement, and maximum allowed drawdown deterioration before results are
  viewed, then report the qualification count for the B cohort.
- Final cohort and comparison definition: Candidate B is eligible only in the
  85% to strictly-less-than-90% original-TP-progress band, with fresh,
  quality-valid 1h `NEUTRAL`, supportive fresh/quality-valid 5m, and required
  current-frame structure/momentum evidence. Missing, stale, invalid, or
  unknown 1h/5m evidence makes the case ineligible rather than non-bearish.
  This avoids changing or competing with existing FIX-11T behaviour at 90%.
  Pre-count the eligible cohort; insufficient sample means keep collecting.
  B-versus-A is the sole primary comparison; C is secondary/exploratory and
  must have its trailing formula fixed before Replay. Verify ATR, spread, and
  tick-size availability as of breach; if historical spread is unavailable,
  use a declared fixed assumption and sensitivity analysis.
- Final breach semantics: eligibility uses `breach_tp_progress`, calculated
  from the executable price at the exact profit-lock breach, not the historic
  high-water mark. Persist/report both `max_tp_progress` and
  `breach_tp_progress`. The continuation emergency floor is distinct from the
  breached original lock; if actual executable price (including modeled
  fee/spread/slippage) already violates that floor, exit immediately. Freeze
  numerical precision, 90%-boundary precedence (FIX-11T owns 90%+), hold
  length, tolerance, floor, costs, sample threshold, and success criteria
  before inspecting results. Report confidence intervals and chronological
  out-of-sample results; a small qualifying cohort is insufficient evidence.
- Team-2 final validity controls: create and commit a numerical pre-registration
  before any outcome is viewed, recording its immutable content hash here.
  Verify available intrabar path data; where tick/price-event evidence cannot
  order TP versus emergency-floor contact, mark the case ambiguous, apply one
  conservative adverse-move-first rule consistently, and report analyses both
  with and without those cases. Compute the floor per trade as
  `max(original_lock - tolerance, fee_adjusted_breakeven)` and report when
  breakeven binds. Compare A and B as paired outcomes on the same eligible
  breach events, reporting per-event B-minus-A and the count of later declines
  avoided by A.

# Replay-versus-Production parity audit Г?" 2026-10-10

- Scope: source-level comparison of the manual Regression/Replay pipeline with
  the current paper-trading Production path. No trading code, configuration,
  database data, or Replay result was changed.
- Confirmed shared authority: Replay regenerates technical indicators through
  `TechnicalIndicatorService`, signals through `AnalysisService`, BUY authority
  through `ExecutionIntelligenceService`, BUY/SELL validation through
  `TradeExecutionValidationService`, and normal exit, TP-continuation,
  profit-lock, and near-TP policies through the corresponding shared policy
  classes. When canonical `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events exist, the Replay
  consumes them in timestamp order before making the same close-candle signal
  visible, matching the documented live ordering.
- Confirmed boundary: Replay is a shadow simulation, not a transactionally
  identical copy of Production. It starts with fixed capital of 10,000 USDT,
  manages one symbol/position in isolation, uses replay-local daily budget and
  position state, and writes only `*_test` tables. It therefore cannot
  reproduce an actual wallet balance, simultaneous positions, global
  `maxOpenPositions`, daily loss limit, idempotency races, database locks, or
  real execution latency.
- Confirmed historical-evidence limitation: derivatives history is not
  persisted, so historical Replay always receives `UNAVAILABLE` derivatives
  context when that feature is enabled. Historical order-book evaluation uses
  stored snapshots only and becomes `UNAVAILABLE` when no snapshot exists,
  whereas live Production samples its in-memory/live Binance source. Such runs
  are useful simulations but are not full signal-parity proof unless the
  required historical evidence exists and is reported.
- Confirmed price-evidence limitation: a Replay run labels itself
  `EXACT_PRICE_REPLAY` whenever at least one accepted price event exists. It
  does not prove complete tick coverage for every time an open position needed
  protection. Windows with no accepted events fall back to signal prices and
  are labelled `SIGNAL_PRICE_FALLBACK`; neither fallback nor partial coverage
  should be described as tick-exact reproduction.
- Test evidence: Java 21 focused tests passed for profit-lock/TP-extension
  parity, position exit/continuation, near-TP policy, and shared sizing.
  Existing Replay-specific coverage is narrow: it does not execute a complete
  Production-versus-Replay scenario across entry, add, wallet controls,
  price-event ordering, partial harvest, and exit.
- Conclusion: Replay is suitable for controlled, manual strategy regression
  and the approved FIX-155 research work only when each run carries explicit
  evidence-quality labels. It is not yet valid to claim it is fully identical
  to Production in all phases. A future parity program must add deterministic
  scenario tests and a run-level eligibility contract before using Replay as a
  release-equivalence certificate.

===== TRADE_INVESTIGATION_AGENT.md =====
# Trade Investigation Agent

## Role

Investigate one Crypto AI Trader trade from durable evidence. Explain what
happened, why the system entered and exited, whether it followed the intended
rules, and whether the entry/exit was supported by the information available
at that time.

Investigations are read-only. Do not change code, configuration, services,
database records, review states, Replay work, or deployment state.

## Required input

Obtain or resolve:

- Environment and application: local, test, or production.
- Database host, port, and schema.
- One trade reference: `paper_position.id`, `wallet_trade.id`, or a symbol plus
  a bounded approximate entry-time window.
- Optional question or suspected issue.

Before analysis, confirm the symbol, trade/position ID, running version or FIX
level, and ingestion mode. Read the actual schema before choosing tables or
columns. Do not assume a table is in `crypto_ai` when it may belong to
`crypto_ai_v2`.

## Evidence rules

- Use configured database and SSH/log connections without displaying secrets.
- Use bounded, read-only queries and targeted log searches.
- Treat stored timestamps as UTC; display both UTC and KSA (`UTC+03:00`).
- Attach record IDs and timestamped log evidence to every factual conclusion.
- Distinguish **confirmed fact**, **supported inference**, and **missing or
  conflicting evidence**.
- Missing logs or records do not prove that no execution/effect occurred.
- A saved signal does not prove execution. A callback return does not prove a
  transaction committed. Under FIX-153, `market_price_event.APPLIED` proves a
  committed successful protection callback, but can be a no-op when there was
  no open position; find the position/trade mutation separately.
- Current configuration does not prove which settings were active at entry. If
  no configuration history exists, report that limitation.

## Investigation workflow

### 1. Resolve identity and environment

Link the trade reference to, where available:

- `paper_position`
- `wallet_trade`
- entry and exit `trade_signal`
- `signal_processing_work`
- wallet/position execution records

Record entry and exit times in UTC and KSA. Confirm whether the trader ran in
local-candle OFF mode or a legacy shared-market mode.

### 2. Collect a small evidence bundle

Start with exact IDs and a narrow time window. Expand only when the evidence
requires it. Collect:

| Evidence | Establishes |
| --- | --- |
| `paper_position` | lifecycle, entry/exit, stop, target, status |
| `wallet_trade` | executed quantity, prices, fees, linked signal |
| `trade_signal` | decision, strategy, scores, entry eligibility, reasons |
| `signal_processing_work` | claim, attempt, completion, expiry, uncertainty |
| `candle` / `technical_indicator` | market data and analysis inputs available then |
| `market_price_event` | intra-candle protection observations and outcomes |
| configuration/version evidence | applicable rules, when historically available |
| application and timing logs | analysis, execution, protection, exceptions, waits |

Avoid fetching large signal explanation fields until they are needed.

### 3. Reconstruct the timeline

Build a timeline, explicitly showing missing stages:

`candle closed Г+' signal saved Г+' work claimed Г+' locks acquired Г+' entry committed Г+' protection observations Г+' exit decision Г+' exit committed`

Measure separately:

- candle close to signal generation;
- signal generation to processing/entry;
- observation time to protection completion;
- exit-trigger evidence to recorded exit.

Correlate delays only with logs/events for the same symbol, position, signal,
or price-event identity. A nearby slow SQL query is not proof of causation.

### 4. Assess entry correctness and quality

Use only evidence that was available at entry. Check:

- signal mode, closed-candle identity, freshness, and data quality;
- final decision and entry-allowed result;
- BTC context, higher-timeframe, ATR, liquidity, risk/reward, sizing, and
  strategy gates that applied at that time;
- correct signal/position linkage and absence of duplicate/stale-retry entry;
- quantity, wallet balance, risk limits, fees, and fill/reference-price gap.

Return two separate conclusions:

- **Execution correctness:** followed or violated the implemented rules.
- **Entry quality:** supported, weak, or unverifiable from contemporaneous
  evidence.

Subsequent price movement may evaluate the outcome; it must never be presented
as information the entry decision had.

### 5. Reconstruct protection and stop-loss behavior

For FIX-153, inspect accepted `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` price events and retain
`PENDING`, `NON_CANONICAL`, and `PROTECTION_NOT_APPLIED` records as evidence
gaps or exclusions. Never substitute candle OHLC for absent intra-candle price
evidence.

For each relevant accepted observation:

1. Determine the then-active stop, target, trailing stop, and profit-lock state.
2. Find the first accepted observation that met an exit threshold.
3. Confirm that protection evaluated it and identify the resulting position or
   wallet execution record.
4. Explain delays, rejections, stale/out-of-order classification, exceptions,
   lock waits, or pool pressure.

Classify the exit path accurately: fixed stop-loss, trailing stop, profit-lock,
take-profit, signal-driven exit, manual exit, or unknown.

### 6. Assess exit correctness and quality

Compare the recorded exit reason, quantity, and price with trigger evidence.
Check threshold reach, accepted-observation identity, protection timing, and
the committed execution. Calculate maximum favourable/adverse movement during
the holding period separately from the contemporaneous exit assessment.

### 7. Reconcile financial results

Recalculate using execution records:

- bought/sold quantities and weighted entry/exit prices;
- gross P&L, fees, and net P&L;
- partial exits, remaining quantity, and realized versus unrealized values.

Flag discrepancies among wallet records, position records, and UI output.

### 8. Diagnose cause and use Replay carefully

Classify findings as one or more of:

- strategy decision;
- data freshness or coverage;
- analysis delay;
- execution failure;
- protection failure;
- database, lock, or connection-pool contention;
- accounting/UI discrepancy;
- insufficient evidence.

Run Replay only as a controlled comparison with equivalent candles, accepted
prices, strategy version, and settings. State every unavailable input or
material difference. Never claim parity by substituting candle OHLC for missing
price evidence or treating `PENDING` as accepted.

## Required final report

1. **Trade identity and environment** Г?" IDs, symbol, version/mode, scope.
2. **UTC/KSA timeline** Г?" evidence-backed sequence and latency breakdown.
3. **Entry** Г?" execution correctness and entry quality.
4. **Exit** Г?" execution correctness and exit quality.
5. **Stop-loss/profit-lock** Г?" worked, failed, or unverifiable, with evidence.
6. **P&L reconciliation** Г?" quantities, prices, fees, and discrepancies.
7. **Root cause and confidence** Г?" confirmed defect, valid loss, configuration
   issue, delay, or insufficient evidence.
8. **Evidence references** Г?" record IDs, query/log timestamps, code/config
   references.
9. **Missing evidence** Г?" exactly what prevents a stronger conclusion.
10. **Recommended correction and regression checks** Г?" only when justified.

A profitable trade can violate the rules, and a losing trade can be a correct
execution. State those independently.

===== TRADING_RULES.md =====
# FIX-154 higher-timeframe BUY rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- `data_quality_valid=true` is required before a bearish 1h signal can be
  treated as trusted. `NULL` is unknown and fails closed for bearish evidence.
- A trusted `STRONG_SELL` at `confidence_score >= 70` blocks the BUY.
- A trusted `SELL`, or trusted `STRONG_SELL` below 70, is a SOFT conflict:
  profiles receive effective 1h `NEUTRAL`, and any allowed initial BUY is
  capped at 25%.
- A bearish 5m still returns `5M_BEARISH_VETO` before any 1h result.
- Existing progressive/add rules retain their raw 1h requirement; a SOFT 1h
  never authorizes an add.

# FIX-153 candle continuity rule Г?" 2026-10-10

- Missing price evidence never becomes invented OHLCV data and never bypasses
  the data-quality gate.
- Automatic reconciliation may restore only one exact, final Binance candle.
  Its recovered/backfilled row has no close token and cannot directly cause
  analysis, a signal, or a trade.
- A later normal live close is the only route that resumes ordinary signal
  generation after a repaired quality window.

## Iteration 1 ƒ?" Trade Reconstruction

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- The six binding subjects are `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutions were made.
- No query-result artifacts exist in the permitted `db/CODEX/` evidence folder. Therefore no trade-specific timeline, P&L reconciliation, entry assessment, or exit/protection conclusion is yet supportable.
- `paper_position` is the authoritative position entity. It contains the entry signal link, optional exit signal link, prices, realized P&L, close reason/reason text, and open/close timestamps. [PaperPosition.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:10)
- `wallet_trade` provides execution-level price, quantity, fee, cost-basis, realized-P&L, status, reason, and observed-price time; it links to a signal, not directly to a paper-position ID. [WalletTrade.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/domain/WalletTrade.java:11)
- `trade_signal` holds the contemporaneous decision, entry gates, strategy/risk fields, price, stop, target, and generated time. [TradeSignal.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/TradeSignal.java:13)
- For protection evidence, only `market_price_event` records in `LEGACY` or `APPLIED` are accepted as canonical price observations; a signal or candle OHLC must not replace absent intrabar price evidence. [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:137)

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- A schema-first evidence request is required before selecting trade-specific fields, because deployed `crypto_ai` schemaƒ?"not source entities or migrationsƒ?"is authoritative.
- Once schema is returned, reconstruction will start from all six `paper_position` rows, then join entry/exit signals and correlate wallet executions by signal, symbol, side, and bounded timestamps. No causal conclusion will be drawn solely from such correlation.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- None.

### UNKNOWNS

- Symbols, entry/exit UTC timestamps, KSA conversions, status, close reasons, prices, and realized P&L for every frozen ID.
- Whether every position has an exit signal and matching wallet execution evidence.
- The actual deployed columns/tables needed for protection, managed-position, audit, candle, indicator, and signal-processing evidence.
- Runtime mode/version/configuration active at each entry.
- Whether exits were stop-loss, target, profit-lock, signal-driven, manual, or unsupported by durable price-event evidence.
- Replay comparability.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I1-001  
Purpose: Read the deployed `crypto_ai` schema before selecting trade-specific evidence. This identifies the actual columns available for the six frozen `paper_position` reconstructions and related signal, execution, protection, audit, work, candle, and indicator evidence.

SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'signal_processing_work',
      'candle',
      'technical_indicator'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
LIMIT 1000;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I2-001  
Purpose: Obtain the deployed schema and the six authoritative position rows while independently re-proving the frozen failed-trade selection. All statements are bounded, read-only, and confined to `crypto_ai`.

SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'signal_processing_work',
      'candle',
      'technical_indicator'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
LIMIT 1000;

SELECT p.*
FROM paper_position p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
LIMIT 6;

SELECT
    id,
    status,
    closed_at,
    realized_pnl
FROM paper_position
WHERE status = 'CLOSED'
  AND closed_at IS NOT NULL
  AND realized_pnl < 0
ORDER BY closed_at DESC
LIMIT 6;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

## Iteration 3 ƒ?" Log and Timing Forensics

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen subjects remain exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutes used.
- The permitted `db/CODEX/` folder is still empty; `DBQ-FTS-001` through `DBQ-FTS-005` results are unavailable.
- The local application log covers only **2026-10-17 00:11:41.424 UTC through 00:26:34.689 UTC**. Its Logback pattern explicitly formats application timestamps in UTC. It is therefore not a complete trade-log source up to the snapshot at **2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC**.
- No local-log line explicitly identifies any frozen `paper_position` ID. This is not exculpatory: normal protection logs identify symbol and sometimes managed-position ID, rather than paper-position ID.
- The direct-price path records `observedUtc`, explicit `observedKsa`, event ID, outcome, and reason when its transaction commits. `APPLIED` means the protection callback transaction committed; it does not alone prove an exit occurred.
- Timing instrumentation measures input, protection, observer, and close-dispatch transactions. It emits a stage record for failures or durations of at least one second; persisted `fix129_stage_timing` diagnostics are bounded/best-effort and can be dropped. It cannot be treated as a complete event ledger.
- No `[FIX-129][STAGE_TIMING]`, `[FIX-129][BLOCK_BEGIN]`, mechanical-exit, or FIX-124 protection-failure entries appear in the available local application-log slice.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- No timing, queueing, lock, stale-price, or protection-delay defect can be attributed to any of the six trades without their symbols, lifecycle timestamps, and corresponding durable event IDs.
- Once position identity is available, the defensible exit-latency measure is:

  `market_price_event.observed_at` ƒ+' matching `wallet_trade.execution_price_observed_at` / `executed_at` ƒ+' `paper_position.closed_at`

  `paper_position.closed_at` is assigned from application `Instant.now()` during closure, so it is a lifecycle-record timestampƒ?"not the exchange observation time.
- The supplied snapshot metadata has a two-second inconsistency: `2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC` converts to `2026-10-18 18:00:04 KSA`, whereas the supplied KSA snapshot is `18:00:02`. I retain both supplied values without normalization.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- None.

### EVIDENCE

- [logback-spring.xml](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/resources/logback-spring.xml:24) formats application-file timestamps as UTC; the inspected log slice ends at `2026-10-17T00:26:34.689`.
- [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:38) preserves input ƒ+' protection ƒ+' observer ƒ+' close-dispatch ordering, uses a 30-second transaction timeout, and permits only the initial position-lock deadlock retry.
- [KlineTiming.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTiming.java:32) uses monotonic elapsed time and records failures/slow stages; [KlineTimingStore.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTimingStore.java:38) makes storage best-effort.
- [MarketPriceEventService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:112) records committed direct-price outcomes with both UTC and KSA observation timestamps.
- [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:54) validates observation age/order under lock and atomically finalizes the price-event outcome; [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391) records paper-position closure using current application time.

## Iteration 4 ƒ?" Code / Trade Route

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen subjects remain exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutions.
- Permitted `db/CODEX/` remains empty. No `DBQ-FTS-001`ƒ?Ý`005` result is available; therefore no trade-specific route, entry/exit cause, or defect is confirmed.
- Current Production entry route is:

  `trade_signal` ƒ+' `SignalProcessingCoordinator` claim/freshness/locks ƒ+' `PaperTradingService` ƒ+' execution-intelligence and validation gates ƒ+' fresh canonical price ƒ+' wallet BUY ƒ+' `paper_position` OPEN.

  The paper position is created only after `WalletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(...)` returns true. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:301)

- Entry uses a fresh canonical market-price event, not `trade_signal.latest_price`, for final revalidation, sizing, wallet execution, and `paper_position.entry_price`. [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:25) [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340)

- The coordinator claims durable work, obtains position locks, rechecks signal freshness, executes the business route transactionally, and records the work outcome. A completed work record does not itself prove a trade was opened. [SignalProcessingCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:43)

- Live-price protection route is:

  canonical price event persisted ƒ+' protection transaction ƒ+' TP / absolute stop / profit-lock / normal multi-timeframe exit / near-TP partial-harvest evaluation.

  Input commits before protection; close-candle dispatch occurs afterward. [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:32)

- For direct live-price exits, a canonical `APPLIED` price event proves the protection callback transaction committed, but it can be a no-op where no position was open. The corresponding wallet and paper-position mutation must be independently established. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:112)

- Live mechanical exit priority is take-profit/continuation, stop-loss, profit-lock, normal exit policy, then near-TP partial harvest. A stop-loss is not softened by continuation logic. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)

- On a profit-lock breach, the route may hold only with specified supportive higher-timeframe conditions; otherwise it produces a `PROFIT_LOCK_*` exit reason. [PositionExitPolicy.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:45)

- `paper_position` carries entry/exit signal links, position lifecycle, prices, P&L, close reason, explanation, and open/close timestamps. [PaperPosition.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:10)

- `wallet_trade` is signal-linked execution evidence, but has no direct `paper_position` foreign key in current source. Mechanical exits may have no signal link. Exact linkage must use deployed records, symbol, reason, quantity, price, and bounded timestamps. [WalletTrade.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/domain/WalletTrade.java:11)

- Current paper calculations preserve prior partial-harvest realized P&L and then add remaining-quantity P&L on terminal close. Current paper wallet executions set fees to zero, so any historical fee discrepancy requires record-level evidence and historical-version confirmation. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391) [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:533)

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- Every selected loss needs two independent reconciliations:  
  1. `paper_position` lifecycle versus entry/exit signals; and  
  2. paper lifecycle versus wallet execution(s) and, where applicable, canonical price-event evidence.  
  A matching symbol alone is insufficient.
- The current route supports several distinct loss pathsƒ?"stop-loss, signal SELL, multi-timeframe exit, profit-lock exit, take-profit/continuation outcome, manual close, or a partial-harvest plus terminal close. No one path can be assigned to a frozen trade without DB evidence.
- Source is current-project evidence, not proof that the same version/configuration was deployed when these six historical trades executed.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- None.

### EVIDENCE

- No trade-specific DB artifact exists in the permitted Codex evidence location.
- No frozen-ID line was available in the local log slice in Iteration 3.
- No raw trade timestamp is available to convert to KSA in this iteration.

### UNKNOWNS

- Each positionƒ?Ts symbol, entry/exit signal IDs, open/close UTC timestamps, close reason, and realized P&L.
- Whether the six losses used live mechanical protection, signal-path closure, manual closure, or a mixed partial-harvest lifecycle.
- Matching wallet BUY/SELL rows, fees, quantities, price-observation timestamps, execution keys, and financial reconciliation.
- Accepted price-event identity and trigger order for any claimed stop-loss, profit-lock, or take-profit exit.
- Signal freshness, data quality, validation decisions, configuration/version, and Replay comparability at each entry.

- Under current source, Entry Quality below 50 blocks the entry as a chase. Scores of 50ƒ?"54, 55ƒ?"69, 70ƒ?"84, and 85+ are execution-eligible but size-capped; BTC conflict, explicit entry authority, and a recent rejection zone can further constrain exposure. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:976)
- The current quality score considers execution-price expansion from a recent/opportunity reference, ATR extension, opportunity age, reward/risk, recent rejection, volatility regime, and stop-exposed liquidity. It is recalculated at execution price. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:865)
- Signal processing claims durable work, obtains the position lock, then freshness-checks before the business route runs. Completion means processing committed, not necessarily that a trade opened. [SignalProcessingCoordinator.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:43)
- `trade_signal` is designed to retain contemporaneous decision, BTC, derivatives, liquidity, regime, strategy, stop/target, ATR, risk/reward, and generated-time evidence. [TradeSignal.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/TradeSignal.java:150)

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- A realized loss does not imply weak entry quality. Each of the six trades remains classified as **unverifiable**, rather than weak or defective, until its contemporaneous signal and execution evidence is available.
- Current source is not proof of the deployed historical version or configuration for these trades. In particular, current FIX-154/data-quality behavior must not be projected backward without record-level timestamps and version/configuration evidence.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- None.

### UNKNOWNS

- Whether each entry was initial, progressive, setup-wakeup, retracement, accumulated-evidence, or another path.

## Iteration 6 ƒ?" Root Cause + Research

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen subjects remain exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**.
- The permitted `db/CODEX/` folder has no query artifacts. Thus no trade-specific root cause, symbol, lifecycle, entry/exit price, close reason, or P&L reconciliation is proven.
- The current position record is designed to hold the required primary evidence: entry/exit prices, realized P&L, entry/exit signal links, close reason, and UTC open/close times. [PaperPosition.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:12)
- Current entry logic obtains a canonical price at or before execution, accepting it only when no more than 15 seconds old; it then revalidates the proposed BUY at that price. [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:24) [PaperTradingService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340)
- Current entry-quality scoring penalizes chase/extension, ATR extension, opportunity age, inadequate reward/risk, rejection-zone proximity, high volatility, and stop-exposed liquidity. It is applied to the execution price rather than merely the signal snapshot. [ExecutionIntelligenceService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionIntelligenceService.java:865)
- Current live exit priority is take-profit, absolute stop-loss, profit-lock, normal multi-timeframe exit, then near-TP handling. A paper close records the protection price, reason, explanation, and application close time. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)
- The project has documented a historical reconnect-boundary failure mode: a missing final closed-kline event can leave candle evidence incomplete and block later analysis. That is a known system risk, **not evidence that any of the six frozen trades was affected**.

**EXTERNAL RESEARCH**

- Binanceƒ?Ts official kline stream documentation describes updates to the current candle and an explicit `x` flag denoting whether the candle is closed; its payload timestamps are UTC. This supports the projectƒ?Ts need to distinguish an observed closed candle from a merely updating candle, but does not establish any historical condition for these trades. [Binance kline stream documentation](https://developers.binance.com/zh-CN/docs/products/spot/testnet/web-socket-streams)

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- No individual loss can be attributed to a strategy defect, late/chase entry, stale-data event, missed protection trigger, fee/accounting issue, or exit-policy decision without the authoritative row and linked evidence.
- Each loss needs this causal test, in order:

  `paper_position lifecycle ƒ+' linked entry/exit signal ƒ+' wallet BUY/SELL reconciliation ƒ+' accepted price-event/protection evidence ƒ+' contemporaneous candles/signals`

- A negative `realized_pnl` alone proves only that the row belongs to the frozen failed-trade selection.
- The supplied snapshot metadata remains internally inconsistent: `2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC` converts to `18:00:04 KSA`, whereas the supplied snapshot states `18:00:02 KSA`. I retain both supplied values; no trade timestamp is presently available to report in KSA.

## Iteration 3 ƒ?" Log and Timing Forensics

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen subjects remain exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutes used.
- The permitted `db/CODEX/` folder is still empty; `DBQ-FTS-001` through `DBQ-FTS-005` results are unavailable.
- The local application log covers only **2026-10-17 00:11:41.424 UTC through 00:26:34.689 UTC**. Its Logback pattern explicitly formats application timestamps in UTC. It is therefore not a complete trade-log source up to the snapshot at **2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC**.
- No local-log line explicitly identifies any frozen `paper_position` ID. This is not exculpatory: normal protection logs identify symbol and sometimes managed-position ID, rather than paper-position ID.
- The direct-price path records `observedUtc`, explicit `observedKsa`, event ID, outcome, and reason when its transaction commits. `APPLIED` means the protection callback transaction committed; it does not alone prove an exit occurred.
- Timing instrumentation measures input, protection, observer, and close-dispatch transactions. It emits a stage record for failures or durations of at least one second; persisted `fix129_stage_timing` diagnostics are bounded/best-effort and can be dropped. It cannot be treated as a complete event ledger.
- No `[FIX-129][STAGE_TIMING]`, `[FIX-129][BLOCK_BEGIN]`, mechanical-exit, or FIX-124 protection-failure entries appear in the available local application-log slice.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- No timing, queueing, lock, stale-price, or protection-delay defect can be attributed to any of the six trades without their symbols, lifecycle timestamps, and corresponding durable event IDs.
- Once position identity is available, the defensible exit-latency measure is:

  `market_price_event.observed_at` ƒ+' matching `wallet_trade.execution_price_observed_at` / `executed_at` ƒ+' `paper_position.closed_at`

  `paper_position.closed_at` is assigned from application `Instant.now()` during closure, so it is a lifecycle-record timestampƒ?"not the exchange observation time.
- The supplied snapshot metadata has a two-second inconsistency: `2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC` converts to `2026-10-18 18:00:04 KSA`, whereas the supplied KSA snapshot is `18:00:02`. I retain both supplied values without normalization.

- [KlineTiming.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTiming.java:32) uses monotonic elapsed time and records failures/slow stages; [KlineTimingStore.java](C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTimingStore.java:38) makes storage best-effort.

## Iteration 4 ƒ?" Code / Trade Route

### FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- Frozen subjects remain exactly `paper_position` IDs **182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137**. No substitutions.
- Permitted `db/CODEX/` remains empty. No `DBQ-FTS-001`ƒ?Ý`005` result is available; therefore no trade-specific route, entry/exit cause, or defect is confirmed.
- Current Production entry route is:

  `trade_signal` ƒ+' `SignalProcessingCoordinator` claim/freshness/locks ƒ+' `PaperTradingService` ƒ+' execution-intelligence and validation gates ƒ+' fresh canonical price ƒ+' wallet BUY ƒ+' `paper_position` OPEN.

  The paper position is created only after `WalletAutoExecutionService.executeBuy(...)` returns true. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:301)

- Entry uses a fresh canonical market-price event, not `trade_signal.latest_price`, for final revalidation, sizing, wallet execution, and `paper_position.entry_price`. [ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionPriceAuthorityService.java:25) [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:340)

- The coordinator claims durable work, obtains position locks, rechecks signal freshness, executes the business route transactionally, and records the work outcome. A completed work record does not itself prove a trade was opened. [SignalProcessingCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/processing/SignalProcessingCoordinator.java:43)

- Live-price protection route is:

  canonical price event persisted ƒ+' protection transaction ƒ+' TP / absolute stop / profit-lock / normal multi-timeframe exit / near-TP partial-harvest evaluation.

  Input commits before protection; close-candle dispatch occurs afterward. [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:32)

- For direct live-price exits, a canonical `APPLIED` price event proves the protection callback transaction committed, but it can be a no-op where no position was open. The corresponding wallet and paper-position mutation must be independently established. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:112)

- Live mechanical exit priority is take-profit/continuation, stop-loss, profit-lock, normal exit policy, then near-TP partial harvest. A stop-loss is not softened by continuation logic. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:173)

- On a profit-lock breach, the route may hold only with specified supportive higher-timeframe conditions; otherwise it produces a `PROFIT_LOCK_*` exit reason. [PositionExitPolicy.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/PositionExitPolicy.java:45)

- `paper_position` carries entry/exit signal links, position lifecycle, prices, P&L, close reason, explanation, and open/close timestamps. [PaperPosition.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/domain/PaperPosition.java:10)

- `wallet_trade` is signal-linked execution evidence, but has no direct `paper_position` foreign key in current source. Mechanical exits may have no signal link. Exact linkage must use deployed records, symbol, reason, quantity, price, and bounded timestamps. [WalletTrade.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/domain/WalletTrade.java:11)

- Current paper calculations preserve prior partial-harvest realized P&L and then add remaining-quantity P&L on terminal close. Current paper wallet executions set fees to zero, so any historical fee discrepancy requires record-level evidence and historical-version confirmation. [LivePositionProtectionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/position/service/LivePositionProtectionService.java:391) [WalletAutoExecutionService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/wallet/service/WalletAutoExecutionService.java:533)

**AGENT INFERENCE**

- Every selected loss needs two independent reconciliations:  
  1. `paper_position` lifecycle versus entry/exit signals; and  
  2. paper lifecycle versus wallet execution(s) and, where applicable, canonical price-event evidence.  
  A matching symbol alone is insufficient.
- The current route supports several distinct loss pathsƒ?"stop-loss, signal SELL, multi-timeframe exit, profit-lock exit, take-profit/continuation outcome, manual close, or a partial-harvest plus terminal close. No one path can be assigned to a frozen trade without DB evidence.
- Source is current-project evidence, not proof that the same version/configuration was deployed when these six historical trades executed.

- None.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I4-001  
Purpose: Establish the deployed schema for the exact entry/exit route and retrieve only the six authoritative positions before requesting route-specific signal, wallet, protection, and audit records. Read-only, bounded, `crypto_ai` only.

SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'position_analysis',
      'signal_processing_work'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
LIMIT 1000;

SELECT p.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
LIMIT 6;
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

# CODEX_FAILED_TRADE_STUDY.md

## Summary

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Track / iteration | CODEX / 8 of 8 — final self-review |
| Study window | WINDOW2 |
| Frozen IDs | 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137 |
| Authoritative entity | `crypto_ai.paper_position` |
| Final classification | **UNVERIFIABLE for all six trades** |
| Confirmed common defect | None |
| Changes made | None — analysis only |

Supplied snapshot metadata is retained verbatim: `2026-10-18 15:00:04 UTC`, supplied `2026-10-18 18:00:02 KSA`, intended window `2026-10-18 18:00:00 KSA`. Arithmetically, the UTC timestamp converts to `2026-10-18 18:00:04 KSA`; this two-second discrepancy is metadata-only and does not change the classification.

## Mandatory Trade Classification

| `paper_position.id` | Selection status | Entry | Exit / protection | P&L reconciliation | Final classification |

| 158 | Mandated frozen ID; not independently DB-proven | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | **UNVERIFIABLE** |
| 143 | Mandated frozen ID; not independently DB-proven | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | **UNVERIFIABLE** |
| 137 | Mandated frozen ID; not independently DB-proven | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | Unverifiable | **UNVERIFIABLE** |

## DATABASE EVIDENCE

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- The permitted Codex-only directory `agent-docs/runs/fts-WINDOW2-20261018-180002/db/CODEX/` exists and contains no result artifacts.
- No results for mandatory proof queries `DBQ-FTS-001` through `DBQ-FTS-005` are available in the permitted evidence scope.
- No database query was successfully available to this analysis. Therefore, none of these are proven for any ID: symbol, entry/close UTC time, realized loss amount, entry/exit signal IDs, close reason, wallet BUY/SELL records, fees, price-event identity, or Replay eligibility.
- The charter’s frozen selection is binding, but it is not independently re-proven without the authoritative query result.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

A negative `realized_pnl` in the selection rule would establish only inclusion in the failed-trade set. It would not establish a bad entry, missed stop, early profit-lock exit, stale data, execution latency, or accounting defect.

## LOG EVIDENCE

**PROJECT EVIDENCE**

- The inspected local application-log slice covers only `2026-10-17 00:11:41.424 UTC` through `2026-10-17 00:26:34.689 UTC`, equivalent to `2026-10-17 03:11:41.424 KSA` through `03:26:34.689 KSA`.
- Logback is configured to format application-file timestamps in UTC.
- No line in that slice identifies frozen `paper_position` IDs 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, or 137.
- No relevant FIX-129 stage-timing, block-begin, mechanical-exit, or FIX-124 protection-failure entry was found in that limited slice.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

This is inadequate coverage for the supplied snapshot on `2026-10-18`; the absence of an ID or error in the slice is not evidence that no execution, protection event, or delay occurred.

## CODE EVIDENCE

**PROJECT EVIDENCE — current working tree only, not historical-runtime proof**

- Current entry flow executes a wallet BUY using the fresh execution price before creating the paper position. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:379)
- Current paper-position creation stores the fresh execution price. [PaperTradingService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/service/PaperTradingService.java:407)
- Current direct-price evidence has a `PENDING` lifecycle and accepts canonical `LEGACY`/`APPLIED` evidence for Replay/price retrieval; an `APPLIED` callback still requires a correlated position/wallet mutation to prove an exit. [MarketPriceEventService.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/market/service/MarketPriceEventService.java:50)
- The current transaction flow preserves input → protection → observer → close-dispatch ordering. [KlineTransactionCoordinator.java](/C:/workspace/crypto-ai-trader-mysql/src/main/java/com/crypto/infrastructure/transaction/KlineTransactionCoordinator.java:32)
- Current source supports multiple loss routes, including stop-loss, profit-lock, normal policy exit, and signal/wallet-driven closure. It does not identify which route applied to any frozen ID.

**AGENT INFERENCE**

Current code is useful only to define the evidence needed for reconstruction. It cannot prove the deployed version, configuration, runtime mode, or behavior at any of the six historical trade times.

## EXTERNAL RESEARCH

A prior iteration referenced Binance’s official kline-stream documentation for the closed-candle flag and UTC payload timing. It provides no trade-specific evidence and carries no weight in this final classification.

## Final Assessment

No confirmed valid loss, strategy defect, data-quality defect, execution failure, protection failure, or accounting discrepancy exists for any frozen trade. The only confirmed finding is an evidence-collection gap: the authorized DB evidence artifacts are absent and the available logs are not a complete trade-correlated record.

No trading-rule or code fix is justified. The only candidate follow-up is evidence acquisition and reconstruction before considering any implementation change.

### DB_REQUEST

QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I8-001  
Purpose: Obtain deployed schema, the six authoritative lifecycle rows, and an independent proof of the frozen selection. Read-only, bounded, `crypto_ai` only.

SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    table_name,
    ordinal_position,
    column_name,
    column_type,
    is_nullable,
    column_key
FROM information_schema.columns
WHERE table_schema = 'crypto_ai'
  AND table_name IN (
      'paper_position',
      'trade_signal',
      'wallet_trade',
      'wallet_managed_position',
      'market_price_event',
      'production_exit_audit',
      'position_management_event',
      'signal_processing_work'
  )
ORDER BY table_name, ordinal_position
LIMIT 1000;

SELECT p.*
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position p
WHERE p.id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(p.id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
LIMIT 6;

SELECT id, status, closed_at, realized_pnl
FROM crypto_ai.paper_position
WHERE status = 'CLOSED'
  AND closed_at IS NOT NULL
  AND realized_pnl < 0
ORDER BY closed_at DESC
LIMIT 6;
```

### END_DB_REQUEST

Documentation-update determination: no update made; this was analysis-only and all writes were prohibited.
